### [77] [AutoScout: Structured Optimization for Automating ML System Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11603)
*Jimmy Shong,Yuhan Ding,Yihan Jiang,Liheng Jing,Haonan Chen,Gaokai Zhang,Aditya Akella,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoScout：一个用于ML训练、微调和推理的通用系统配置器，通过混合优化框架解决异构配置空间问题，实现2.7-3.0倍训练加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML系统配置空间快速扩展，包含模型并行策略、通信优化和运行时参数等。端到端系统效率对这些选择高度敏感，但由于异构特征类型、条件依赖和高昂的搜索成本，识别高性能配置具有挑战性。现有方法要么优化有限的配置维度，要么依赖无法随配置空间增长而泛化的启发式方法。

Method: 将系统配置建模为具有层次依赖关系的混合离散/连续优化问题，引入混合优化框架联合优化稀疏结构决策和密集执行参数。为降低分析成本，自适应地优先处理高影响配置特征，并集成不同保真度的模拟器。

Result: 在多样化模型、硬件平台和部署目标上，AutoScout始终能识别出高性能配置，相比专家调优设置实现了2.7-3.0倍的训练加速。

Conclusion: AutoScout是一个通用的ML系统配置器，通过混合优化框架有效解决了复杂配置空间的优化问题，显著提升了ML训练、微调和推理的系统效率。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) systems expose a rapidly expanding configuration space spanning model-parallelism strategies, communication optimizations, and low-level runtime parameters. End-to-end system efficiency is highly sensitive to these choices, yet identifying high-performance configurations is challenging due to heterogeneous feature types (e.g., sparse and dense parameters), conditional dependencies (e.g., valid execution parameters only under specific upstream decisions), and the high search (profiling) cost. Existing approaches either optimize a narrow subset of configuration dimensions or rely on ad-hoc heuristics that fail to generalize as configuration spaces continue to grow. We present AutoScout, a general-purpose systems configurator for ML training, fine-tuning, and inference. It formulates the system configuration as a mixed-discrete/continuous optimization problem with hierarchical dependencies and introduces a hybrid optimization framework that jointly refines sparse structural decisions and dense execution parameters. To reduce profiling cost, AutoScout adaptively prioritizes high-impact configuration features and ensembles simulators with varying fidelity. Across diverse models, hardware platforms, and deployment objectives, AutoScout consistently identifies high-performance configurations, achieving 2.7-3.0$\times$ training speedup over expert-tuned settings.

</details>


### [78] [Fractional Rotation, Full Potential? Investigating Performance and Convergence of Partial RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11611)
*Mohammad Aflah Khan,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 部分RoPE（旋转位置编码）研究：仅对约10%的隐藏维度应用RoPE即可达到与完整RoPE相当的收敛效果，实现高达10倍的内存节省，同时保持训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究部分RoPE（仅对部分隐藏维度应用旋转位置编码）的效果，探索在保持模型性能的同时减少内存占用的方法。特别关注长上下文场景下的内存节省潜力，以及不同RoPE比例对训练动态和收敛的影响。

Method: 系统研究部分RoPE对训练动态和收敛的影响，包括：1）在不同比例（从0%到100%）的隐藏维度上应用RoPE；2）跨不同模型规模、序列长度和数据集进行实验；3）比较NoPE（无位置编码）、部分RoPE和完整RoPE的性能差异；4）分析训练稳定性问题及缓解方法（如QK-Norm）。

Result: 1）仅对约10%的隐藏维度应用RoPE即可达到与完整RoPE相当的收敛效果；2）这一趋势在不同模型规模、序列长度和数据集上保持一致，高质量数据带来更低的总体损失和相似的基准性能；3）NoPE模型展示出不稳定的学习轨迹，可通过最小化RoPE应用或QK-Norm缓解，但后者收敛到更高的损失；4）相比标准RoPE缓存，部分RoPE可实现高达10倍的内存节省。

Conclusion: 部分RoPE是一种有效的设计选择，能够在保持模型性能的同时显著减少内存占用。仅对约10%的隐藏维度应用RoPE即可获得与完整RoPE相当的收敛效果，为模型设计者提供了平衡效率和训练稳定性的实用指导。研究强调了部分RoPE的重要性，特别是在长上下文场景下的内存节省潜力。

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is a common choice in transformer architectures for encoding relative positional information. Although earlier work has examined omitting RoPE in specific layers, the effect of varying the fraction of hidden dimensions that receive rotary transformations remains largely unexplored. This design choice can yield substantial memory savings, which becomes especially significant at long context lengths. We find up to 10x memory savings over the standard RoPE cache, while achieving comparable final loss. In this work, we present a systematic study examining the impact of partial RoPE on training dynamics and convergence across architectures and datasets. Our findings uncover several notable patterns: (1) applying RoPE to only a small fraction of dimensions (around 10%) achieves convergence comparable to using full RoPE; (2) these trends hold consistently across model size, sequence lengths and datasets of varying quality and architectures, with higher-quality data resulting in lower overall loss and similar benchmark performance; and (3) some models trained with NoPE (No Positional Encoding) showcase unstable learning trajectories, which can be alleviated through minimal RoPE application or QK-Norm which converges to a higher loss. Together, these results offer practical guidance for model designers aiming to balance efficiency and training stability, while emphasizing the previously overlooked importance of partial RoPE.

</details>


### [79] [Personalized Federated Learning via Gaussian Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11620)
*Peng Hu,Jianwei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pFedGM是一种基于高斯生成建模的个性化联邦学习方法，通过建模客户端异质性并采用双目标优化，在全局协作与个性化之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习方法主要关注分类器头引导的个性化，忽略了表示分布中的个性化特征。需要一种能够同时建模客户端异质性并平衡全局协作与个性化的方法。

Method: 基于高斯生成建模，首先训练高斯生成器通过加权重采样建模客户端异质性。采用双目标优化：共享目标最大化客户端间类间距离，局部目标最小化客户端内类内距离。将高斯分类器解耦为全局优化的导航器和捕获分布统计量的统计提取器。采用受卡尔曼增益启发的双尺度融合框架，将全局表示分布建模为先验，客户端特定数据建模为似然，通过贝叶斯推理进行类别概率估计。

Result: 在多种场景下进行评估：类数量异质性、环境损坏、多个基准数据集和配置。pFedGM相比最先进方法实现了优越或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: pFedGM通过高斯生成建模有效解决了联邦学习中的数据异质性问题，在保持全局协作的同时实现了更好的个性化，在多种场景下表现出色。

Abstract: Federated learning has emerged as a paradigm to train models collaboratively on inherently distributed client data while safeguarding privacy. In this context, personalized federated learning tackles the challenge of data heterogeneity by equipping each client with a dedicated model. A prevalent strategy decouples the model into a shared feature extractor and a personalized classifier head, where the latter actively guides the representation learning. However, previous works have focused on classifier head-guided personalization, neglecting the potential personalized characteristics in the representation distribution. Building on this insight, we propose pFedGM, a method based on Gaussian generative modeling. The approach begins by training a Gaussian generator that models client heterogeneity via weighted re-sampling. A balance between global collaboration and personalization is then struck by employing a dual objective: a shared objective that maximizes inter-class distance across clients, and a local objective that minimizes intra-class distance within them. To achieve this, we decouple the conventional Gaussian classifier into a navigator for global optimization, and a statistic extractor for capturing distributional statistics. Inspired by the Kalman gain, the algorithm then employs a dual-scale fusion framework at global and local levels to equip each client with a personalized classifier head. In this framework, we model the global representation distribution as a prior and the client-specific data as the likelihood, enabling Bayesian inference for class probability estimation. The evaluation covers a comprehensive range of scenarios: heterogeneity in class counts, environmental corruption, and multiple benchmark datasets and configurations. pFedGM achieves superior or competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [80] [Simple Recipe Works: Vision-Language-Action Models are Natural Continual Learners with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11653)
*Jiaheng Hu,Jay Shim,Chen Tang,Yoonchang Sung,Bo Liu,Peter Stone,Roberto Martin-Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在视觉-语言-动作模型的持续强化学习中，简单的顺序微调配合低秩适配表现优异，挑战了传统持续学习需要复杂方法避免灾难性遗忘的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习认为顺序微调会导致灾难性遗忘，需要复杂的持续学习策略。本研究旨在系统评估大型预训练视觉-语言-动作模型在持续强化学习中的表现，重新审视这一传统认知。

Method: 在三个模型和五个具有挑战性的终身强化学习基准上，对大型预训练视觉-语言-动作模型进行持续强化学习的系统研究，采用简单的顺序微调配合低秩适配方法。

Result: 简单顺序微调配合低秩适配表现出色：具有高可塑性、几乎没有遗忘、保持强大的零样本泛化能力，经常优于更复杂的持续强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 顺序微调是视觉-语言-动作模型持续强化学习的强大方法，大型预训练模型、参数高效适配和策略强化学习的协同作用重塑了稳定性-可塑性权衡，为大规模模型时代的终身学习提供了新见解。

Abstract: Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is a promising direction toward self-improving embodied agents that can adapt in openended, evolving environments. However, conventional wisdom from continual learning suggests that naive Sequential Fine-Tuning (Seq. FT) leads to catastrophic forgetting, necessitating complex CRL strategies. In this work, we take a step back and conduct a systematic study of CRL for large pretrained VLAs across three models and five challenging lifelong RL benchmarks. We find that, contrary to established belief, simple Seq. FT with low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is remarkably strong: it achieves high plasticity, exhibits little to no forgetting, and retains strong zero-shot generalization, frequently outperforming more sophisticated CRL methods. Through detailed analysis, we show that this robustness arises from a synergy between the large pretrained model, parameter-efficient adaptation, and on-policy RL. Together, these components reshape the stability-plasticity trade-off, making continual adaptation both stable and scalable. Our results position Sequential Fine-Tuning as a powerful method for continual RL with VLAs and provide new insights into lifelong learning in the large model era. Code is available at github.com/UT-Austin-RobIn/continual-vla-rl.

</details>


### [81] [Chem4DLLM: 4D Multimodal LLMs for Chemical Dynamics Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11924)
*Xinyu Li,Zhen Zhang,Qi Chen,Anton van den Hengel,Lina Yao,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出化学动力学理解新任务ChemDU，将4D分子轨迹转化为自然语言解释，构建首个数据集Chem4DBench，并开发统一模型Chem4DLLM整合等变图编码器与大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学理解任务主要依赖静态分子表示，无法建模化学键断裂、构象变化等动态现象，而这些动态过程对理解化学反应机理至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出ChemDU新任务：将4D分子轨迹转化为可解释的自然语言描述；2) 构建Chem4DBench数据集：包含4D分子轨迹与专家撰写的解释；3) 开发Chem4DLLM模型：整合等变图编码器（捕获分子几何与旋转动态）与预训练大语言模型。

Result: 建立了首个面向化学动力学理解的数据集和基准，提出了统一的多模态模型框架，为动态化学理解和科学推理研究提供了新方向。

Conclusion: ChemDU任务、Chem4DBench数据集和Chem4DLLM模型共同推动了动态化学理解研究，有望促进多模态科学推理的发展。

Abstract: Existing chemical understanding tasks primarily rely on static molecular representations, limiting their ability to model inherently dynamic phenomena such as bond breaking or conformational changes, which are essential for a chemist to understand chemical reactions. To address this gap, we introduce Chemical Dynamics Understanding (ChemDU), a new task that translates 4D molecular trajectories into interpretable natural-language explanations. ChemDU focuses on fundamental dynamic scenarios, including gas-phase and catalytic reactions, and requires models to reason about key events along molecular trajectories, such as bond formation and dissociation, and to generate coherent, mechanistically grounded narratives. To benchmark this capability, we construct Chem4DBench, the first dataset pairing 4D molecular trajectories with expert-authored explanations across these settings. We further propose Chem4DLLM, a unified model that integrates an equivariant graph encoder with a pretrained large language model to explicitly capture molecular geometry and rotational dynamics. We hope that ChemDU, together with Chem4DBench and Chem4DLLM, will stimulate further research in dynamic chemical understanding and multimodal scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [82] [Context-dependent manifold learning: A neuromodulated constrained autoencoder approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11673)
*Jérôme Adriaens,Guillaume Drion,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经调节约束自编码器（NcAE），通过增益和偏置调节机制实现上下文依赖的流形学习，解决传统约束自编码器无法适应变化物理参数的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束自编码器（cAE）虽然能通过强制几何结构实现可解释的降维，但无法适应变化的物理参数或环境条件，容易将上下文变化与主要输入混淆

Method: 将神经调节机制集成到cAE框架中，提出NcAE架构，通过基于静态上下文信息的增益和偏置调节来自适应参数化几何约束

Result: 在动力系统上的实验表明，NcAE能准确捕捉不同状态下流形几何的变化，同时保持严格的投影特性，有效解耦全局上下文参数与局部流形表示

Conclusion: 神经调节机制为开发更灵活、物理信息化的表示提供了基础，特别适用于受非平稳环境约束的系统

Abstract: Constrained autoencoders (cAE) provide a successful path towards interpretable dimensionality reduction by enforcing geometric structure on latent spaces. However, standard cAEs cannot adapt to varying physical parameters or environmental conditions without conflating these contextual shifts with the primary input. To address this, we integrated a neuromodulatory mechanism into the cAE framework to allow for context-dependent manifold learning. This paper introduces the Neuromodulated Constrained Autoencoder (NcAE), which adaptively parameterizes geometric constraints via gain and bias tuning conditioned on static contextual information. Experimental results on dynamical systems show that the NcAE accurately captures how manifold geometry varies across different regimes while maintaining rigorous projection properties. These results demonstrate that neuromodulation effectively decouples global contextual parameters from local manifold representations. This architecture provides a foundation for developing more flexible, physics-informed representations in systems subject to (non-stationary) environmental constraints.

</details>


### [83] [Entropy-Preserving Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11682)
*Aleksei Petrenko,Ben Lipkin,Kevin Chen,Erik Wijmans,Marco Cusumano-Towner,Raja Giryes,Philipp Krähenbühl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了策略梯度算法在语言模型推理中的熵减少问题，提出了监控和控制训练过程中熵的方法，包括REPO和ADAPO算法来保持轨迹多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略梯度算法在语言模型推理中取得了重要进展，但许多算法在训练过程中会自然减少熵，从而降低探索轨迹的多样性，导致策略的探索能力受限。作者认为需要主动监控和控制训练过程中的熵。

Method: 1. 形式化分析主要策略梯度目标对熵动态的贡献；2. 识别影响熵行为的经验因素（如数值精度）；3. 提出明确的熵控制机制：REPO（通过修改优势函数调节熵的算法族）和ADAPO（自适应非对称裁剪方法）。

Result: 使用熵保持方法训练的模型在整个训练过程中保持多样性，产生的最终策略性能更好，并且在新环境中进行顺序学习时保持可训练性。

Conclusion: 在策略梯度训练中主动监控和控制熵对于保持轨迹多样性和探索能力至关重要，提出的REPO和ADAPO方法能够有效维持熵水平，提升模型性能和持续学习能力。

Abstract: Policy gradient algorithms have driven many recent advancements in language model reasoning. An appealing property is their ability to learn from exploration on their own trajectories, a process crucial for fostering diverse and creative solutions. As we show in this paper, many policy gradient algorithms naturally reduce the entropy -- and thus the diversity of explored trajectories -- as part of training, yielding a policy increasingly limited in its ability to explore. In this paper, we argue that entropy should be actively monitored and controlled throughout training. We formally analyze the contributions of leading policy gradient objectives on entropy dynamics, identify empirical factors (such as numerical precision) that significantly impact entropy behavior, and propose explicit mechanisms for entropy control. These include REPO, a family of algorithms that modify the advantage function to regulate entropy, and ADAPO, an adaptive asymmetric clipping approach. Models trained with our entropy-preserving methods maintain diversity throughout training, yielding final policies that are more performant and retain their trainability for sequential learning in new environments.

</details>


### [84] [Implicit Statistical Inference in Transformers: Approximating Likelihood-Ratio Tests In-Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10573)
*Faris Chaudhry,Siddhant Gadkari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过统计决策理论视角研究Transformer的上下文学习机制，发现模型能够近似贝叶斯最优充分统计量，而非简单的相似性匹配


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管上下文学习（ICL）允许Transformer无需权重更新即可适应新任务，但其底层算法机制仍不明确。研究者希望从统计决策理论角度理解ICCL的工作原理，特别是在已知算法真值的数学严谨框架下进行机制可解释性分析。

Method: 采用统计决策理论视角，研究简单的二元假设检验问题（最优策略由似然比检验决定）。训练Transformer处理需要不同几何结构的任务：线性平移均值估计与非线性方差估计。利用已知的算法真值，通过logit lens和电路对齐进行机制分析。

Result: Transformer能够从上下文中近似贝叶斯最优充分统计量（达到单调变换的程度），在非线性区域匹配理想oracle估计器的性能。机制分析表明模型不依赖固定的核平滑启发式方法，而是自适应地调整决策的线性可分点：线性任务呈现投票式集成模式，非线性任务则使用更深层的顺序计算。

Conclusion: 上下文学习源于任务自适应统计估计器的构建，而非简单的相似性匹配。模型能够根据任务几何结构自适应地调整计算策略，近似最优统计决策过程。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) allows Transformers to adapt to novel tasks without weight updates, yet the underlying algorithms remain poorly understood. We adopt a statistical decision-theoretic perspective by investigating simple binary hypothesis testing, where the optimal policy is determined by the likelihood-ratio test. Notably, this setup provides a mathematically rigorous setting for mechanistic interpretability where the target algorithmic ground truth is known. By training Transformers on tasks requiring distinct geometries (linear shifted means vs. nonlinear variance estimation), we demonstrate that the models approximate the Bayes-optimal sufficient statistics from context up to some monotonic transformation, matching the performance of an ideal oracle estimator in nonlinear regimes. Leveraging this analytical ground truth, mechanistic analysis via logit lens and circuit alignment suggests that the model does not rely on a fixed kernel smoothing heuristic. Instead, it appears to adapt the point at which decisions become linearly decodable: exhibiting patterns consistent with a voting-style ensemble for linear tasks while utilizing a deeper sequential computation for nonlinear tasks. These findings suggest that ICL emerges from the construction of task-adaptive statistical estimators rather than simple similarity matching.

</details>


### [85] [EvoFlows: Evolutionary Edit-Based Flow-Matching for Protein Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11703)
*Nicolas Deutschmann,Constance Ferragu,Jonathan D. Ziegler,Shayan Aziznejad,Eli Bixby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoFlows是一种用于蛋白质工程的变长序列到序列建模方法，通过可控的插入、删除和替换操作预测蛋白质突变的位置和类型，利用编辑流学习进化相关蛋白质序列之间的突变轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归和掩码语言模型在蛋白质工程中存在局限性，需要一种能够同时预测突变类型和位置的方法，以更好地模拟蛋白质进化过程并生成自然且非平凡的突变体。

Method: EvoFlows采用变长序列到序列建模框架，通过编辑流学习进化相关蛋白质序列之间的突变轨迹，实现对模板蛋白质序列进行有限且可控的插入、删除和替换操作。

Result: 在UNIREF和OAS的多样化蛋白质群落上进行大量计算机评估，显示EvoFlows在捕获蛋白质序列分布方面与主流掩码语言模型质量相当，同时在从给定模板生成非平凡但自然样突变体方面表现出改进能力。

Conclusion: EvoFlows为蛋白质工程提供了一种独特且有效的建模方法，能够更好地模拟蛋白质进化过程，生成高质量的自然样突变体，在蛋白质工程应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: We introduce EvoFlows, a variable-length sequence-to-sequence protein modeling approach uniquely suited to protein engineering. Unlike autoregressive and masked language models, EvoFlows perform a limited, controllable number of insertions, deletions, and substitutions on a template protein sequence. In other words, EvoFlows predict not only _which_ mutation to perform, but also _where_ it should occur. Our approach leverages edit flows to learn mutational trajectories between evolutionarily-related protein sequences, simultaneously modeling distributions of related natural proteins and the mutational paths connecting them. Through extensive _in silico_ evaluation on diverse protein communities from UNIREF and OAS, we demonstrate that EvoFlows capture protein sequence distributions with a quality comparable to leading masked language models commonly used in protein engineering, while showing improved ability to generate non-trivial yet natural-like mutants from a given template protein.

</details>


### [86] [Mitigating the Multiplicity Burden: The Role of Calibration in Reducing Predictive Multiplicity of Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11750)
*Mustafa Cavus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了机器学习模型在信用风险评估中分类校准与预测多样性之间的相互作用，发现少数类样本承担了不成比例的多样性负担，而事后校准方法（特别是Platt Scaling和Isotonic Regression）能有效降低Rashomon集合中的预测多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习模型在高风险环境中的部署日益增多，确保概率可靠性和预测稳定性变得至关重要。本研究旨在解决分类校准与预测多样性（即Rashomon集合中多个近最优模型对同一申请人产生冲突信用结果的现象）之间的相互作用问题。

Method: 使用九个不同的信用风险基准数据集，研究预测多样性是否集中在低预测置信度区域，以及事后校准如何减轻算法任意性。实证分析了少数类观测值的多样性负担，并比较了三种事后校准方法：Platt Scaling、Isotonic Regression和Temperature Scaling。

Result: 实证分析显示：1）少数类观测值承担了不成比例的多样性负担，预测多样性和预测置信度存在显著差异；2）应用事后校准方法与Rashomon集合中较低的模糊度相关；3）在测试的技术中，Platt Scaling和Isotonic Regression对降低预测多样性提供了最稳健的减少效果。

Conclusion: 校准可以作为共识强制层，通过减轻预测多样性来支持程序公平性。研究结果表明，事后校准方法特别是Platt Scaling和Isotonic Regression，能够有效降低信用风险评估中的预测多样性，从而减少算法任意性。

Abstract: As machine learning models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes environments, ensuring both probabilistic reliability and prediction stability has become critical. This paper examines the interplay between classification calibration and predictive multiplicity - the phenomenon in which multiple near-optimal models within the Rashomon set yield conflicting credit outcomes for the same applicant. Using nine diverse credit risk benchmark datasets, we investigate whether predictive multiplicity concentrates in regions of low predictive confidence and how post-hoc calibration can mitigate algorithmic arbitrariness. Our empirical analysis reveals that minority class observations bear a disproportionate multiplicity burden, as confirmed by significant disparities in predictive multiplicity and prediction confidence. Furthermore, our empirical comparisons indicate that applying post-hoc calibration methods - specifically Platt Scaling, Isotonic Regression, and Temperature Scaling - is associated with lower obscurity across the Rashomon set. Among the tested techniques, Platt Scaling and Isotonic Regression provide the most robust reduction in predictive multiplicity. These findings suggest that calibration can function as a consensus-enforcing layer and may support procedural fairness by mitigating predictive multiplicity.

</details>


### [87] [A Further Efficient Algorithm with Best-of-Both-Worlds Guarantees for $m$-Set Semi-Bandit Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11764)
*Botao Chen,Jongyeong Lee,Chansoo Kim,Junya Honda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FTPL算法在m-set半赌博问题中达到最优遗憾界，同时实现对抗和随机环境下的最佳性能，并通过条件几何重采样降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTPL作为对抗组合半赌博问题中具有前景的高效算法，其最优性一直未知，而FTRL的最优性已在多种在线学习任务中得到证明。本文旨在研究FTPL在m-set半赌博问题中的最优性和计算复杂度。

Method: 将带有几何重采样(GR)的FTPL分析扩展到m-set半赌博问题（组合半赌博的特殊情况）。使用特定参数的Fréchet和Pareto分布，并扩展条件几何重采样到m-set半赌博，以在FTPL中实现高效损失估计。

Result: 在对抗设置中，使用特定参数的Fréchet和Pareto分布的FTPL实现了最优遗憾界O(√(mdT))。在随机设置中，使用特定参数的FTPL实现了对数遗憾，表明FTPL在m-set半赌博问题中具有"两全其美"的最优性。条件几何重采样将计算复杂度从原始几何重采样的O(d²)降低到O(md(log(d/m)+1))，而不牺牲遗憾性能。

Conclusion: FTPL在m-set半赌博问题中达到了最优遗憾界，同时实现了对抗和随机环境下的最佳性能，并通过条件几何重采样显著降低了计算复杂度，证明了FTPL在该问题中的最优性和高效性。

Abstract: This paper studies the optimality and complexity of Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) policy in $m$-set semi-bandit problems. FTPL has been studied extensively as a promising candidate of an efficient algorithm with favorable regret for adversarial combinatorial semi-bandits. Nevertheless, the optimality of FTPL has still been unknown unlike Follow-the-Regularized-Leader (FTRL) whose optimality has been proved for various tasks of online learning. In this paper, we extend the analysis of FTPL with geometric resampling (GR) to $m$-set semi-bandits, which is a special case of combinatorial semi-bandits, showing that FTPL with Fréchet and Pareto distributions with certain parameters achieves the best possible regret of $O(\sqrt{mdT})$ in adversarial setting. We also show that FTPL with Fréchet and Pareto distributions with a certain parameter achieves a logarithmic regret for stochastic setting, meaning the Best-of-Both-Worlds optimality of FTPL for $m$-set semi-bandit problems. Furthermore, we extend the conditional geometric resampling to $m$-set semi-bandits for efficient loss estimation in FTPL, reducing the computational complexity from $O(d^2)$ of the original geometric resampling to $O(md(\log(d/m)+1))$ without sacrificing the regret performance.

</details>


### [88] [Language Generation with Replay: A Learning-Theoretic View of Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11784)
*Giorgio Racca,Michal Valko,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从学习理论角度研究模型崩溃问题，通过语言生成极限框架分析生成文本回放对LLM训练的影响，发现回放对最强生成概念无害，但对较弱概念产生分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM训练数据需求增长，公开在线文本可能被耗尽，同时机器生成内容增加，导致生成文本可能重新进入训练数据，引发模型崩溃风险。现有实践方法缺乏理论分析，需要从学习理论角度研究此问题。

Method: 使用语言生成极限框架，引入回放对手概念，将生成器过去的输出添加到示例流中。通过理论分析不同生成概念（均匀生成、非均匀生成、极限生成）对回放的敏感性。

Result: 回放对最强的均匀生成概念是良性的，但对较弱的非均匀生成和极限生成概念会产生可证明的分离。正面结果反映了实践中广泛使用的启发式方法（数据清洗、水印、输出过滤），而分离结果显示了这些方法可能失败的情况。

Conclusion: 模型崩溃问题需要从学习理论角度理解，回放对手对生成能力的影响取决于生成概念的强度。研究为实践中数据管理策略提供了理论依据，并指出了现有方法可能失效的边界条件。

Abstract: As scaling laws push the training of frontier large language models (LLMs) toward ever-growing data requirements, training pipelines are approaching a regime where much of the publicly available online text may be consumed. At the same time, widespread LLM usage increases the volume of machine-generated content on the web; together, these trends raise the likelihood of generated text re-entering future training corpora, increasing the associated risk of performance degradation often called model collapse. In practice, model developers address this concern through data cleaning, watermarking, synthetic-data policies, or, in some cases, blissful ignorance. However, the problem of model collapse in generative models has not been examined from a learning-theoretic perspective: we study it through the theoretical lens of the language generation in the limit framework, introducing a replay adversary that augments the example stream with the generator's own past outputs. Our main contribution is a fine-grained learning-theoretic characterization of when replay fundamentally limits generation: while replay is benign for the strongest notion of uniform generation, it provably creates separations for the weaker notions of non-uniform generation and generation in the limit. Interestingly, our positive results mirror heuristics widely used in practice, such as data cleaning, watermarking, and output filtering, while our separations show when these ideas can fail.

</details>


### [89] [Disentangled Representation Learning through Unsupervised Symmetry Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11790)
*Dang-Nhu Barthélémy,Annabi Louis,Argentieri Sylvain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过智能体自主交互发现动作空间群结构的方法，无需强先验知识或限制性子群假设，实现线性对称解耦表示学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对称性的解耦表示学习方法需要强先验的群结构知识或限制性的子群假设，限制了方法的通用性和实用性

Method: 智能体通过无监督环境交互自主发现动作空间的群结构，提出两种算法：1)从交互数据中发现群分解的算法；2)无需特定子群假设的线性对称解耦表示学习算法

Result: 在三个具有不同群分解结构的环境中验证了方法的有效性，性能优于现有的LSBD方法

Conclusion: 该方法在最小假设下证明了真实对称群分解的可识别性，实现了无需强先验知识的对称解耦表示学习，具有更好的通用性

Abstract: Symmetry-based disentangled representation learning leverages the group structure of environment transformations to uncover the latent factors of variation. Prior approaches to symmetry-based disentanglement have required strong prior knowledge of the symmetry group's structure, or restrictive assumptions about the subgroup properties. In this work, we remove these constraints by proposing a method whereby an embodied agent autonomously discovers the group structure of its action space through unsupervised interaction with the environment. We prove the identifiability of the true symmetry group decomposition under minimal assumptions, and derive two algorithms: one for discovering the group decomposition from interaction data, and another for learning Linear Symmetry-Based Disentangled (LSBD) representations without assuming specific subgroup properties. Our method is validated on three environments exhibiting different group decompositions, where it outperforms existing LSBD approaches.

</details>


### [90] [Exponential-Family Membership Inference: From LiRA and RMIA to BaVarIA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11799)
*Rickard Brännvall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个统一框架，将LiRA、RMIA和BASE三种成员推理攻击方法统一为指数族对数似然比框架的不同实例，揭示了它们之间的层次关系，并提出了BaVarIA攻击方法来解决小影子模型预算下的方差估计瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推理攻击方法（LiRA、RMIA、BASE）看似使用不同的评分策略，且BASE被证明等价于RMIA，这使得实践者难以选择合适的方法。需要统一理解这些攻击方法之间的关系，并解决小影子模型预算下的性能瓶颈问题。

Method: 1. 提出指数族对数似然比统一框架，将LiRA、RMIA和BASE视为该框架的不同实例，仅区别在于分布假设和每个数据点估计的参数数量；2. 建立BASE1-4层次结构，连接RMIA和LiRA作为模型复杂度谱系的端点；3. 识别方差估计为小影子模型预算下的关键瓶颈；4. 提出BaVarIA（贝叶斯方差推理攻击），使用共轭正态-逆伽马先验替代基于阈值的参数切换；5. 生成Student-t预测（BaVarIA-t）或具有稳定方差的Gaussian（BaVarIA-n）。

Result: 在12个数据集和7种影子模型预算下，BaVarIA匹配或优于LiRA和RMIA，在实践重要的低影子模型和离线场景中获得最大性能提升。BaVarIA-t和BaVarIA-n提供稳定性能，无需额外超参数调优。

Conclusion: 该研究统一了主要成员推理攻击方法，揭示了它们的内在联系，并通过贝叶斯方差推理解决了小影子模型预算下的性能瓶颈。BaVarIA在实际应用中具有显著优势，特别是在资源受限的场景下。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are becoming standard tools for auditing the privacy of machine learning models. The leading attacks -- LiRA (Carlini et al., 2022) and RMIA (Zarifzadeh et al., 2024) -- appear to use distinct scoring strategies, while the recently proposed BASE (Lassila et al., 2025) was shown to be equivalent to RMIA, making it difficult for practitioners to choose among them. We show that all three are instances of a single exponential-family log-likelihood ratio framework, differing only in their distributional assumptions and the number of parameters estimated per data point. This unification reveals a hierarchy (BASE1-4) that connects RMIA and LiRA as endpoints of a spectrum of increasing model complexity. Within this framework, we identify variance estimation as the key bottleneck at small shadow-model budgets and propose BaVarIA, a Bayesian variance inference attack that replaces threshold-based parameter switching with conjugate normal-inverse-gamma priors. BaVarIA yields a Student-t predictive (BaVarIA-t) or a Gaussian with stabilized variance (BaVarIA-n), providing stable performance without additional hyperparameter tuning. Across 12 datasets and 7 shadow-model budgets, BaVarIA matches or improves upon LiRA and RMIA, with the largest gains in the practically important low-shadow-model and offline regimes.

</details>


### [91] [On the Role of Reversible Instance Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11869)
*Gaspard Berthelier,Tahar Nabil,Etienne Le Naour,Richard Niamke,Samir Perlaza,Giovanni Neglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视时间序列预测中的归一化方法，识别了三个核心挑战，并通过消融实验发现Reversible Instance Normalization (RevIN)的多个组件是冗余甚至有害的，提出了改进RevIN鲁棒性和泛化能力的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据归一化是深度学习模型的关键组成部分，但在时间序列预测中的作用尚未得到充分理解。作者旨在解决时间序列预测中归一化面临的三个核心挑战：时间输入分布偏移、空间输入分布偏移和条件输出分布偏移。

Method: 通过消融研究重新审视广泛使用的Reversible Instance Normalization (RevIN)，分析其各个组件的有效性，识别冗余或有害的组件。

Result: 研究发现RevIN的多个组件是冗余的甚至对性能有负面影响，这为改进RevIN提供了新的见解。

Conclusion: 基于观察结果，作者提出了改进RevIN鲁棒性和泛化能力的新视角，为时间序列预测中的归一化方法提供了更深入的理解和改进方向。

Abstract: Data normalization is a crucial component of deep learning models, yet its role in time series forecasting remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we identify three central challenges for normalization in time series forecasting: temporal input distribution shift, spatial input distribution shift, and conditional output distribution shift. In this context, we revisit the widely used Reversible Instance Normalization (RevIN), by showing through ablation studies that several of its components are redundant or even detrimental. Based on these observations, we draw new perspectives to improve RevIN's robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [92] [Causal Representation Learning with Optimal Compression under Complex Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11907)
*Wanting Liang,Haoang Chi,Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解决多治疗场景下个体治疗效果估计的两个关键挑战：平衡权重超参数选择困境和计算可扩展性的维度诅咒。通过理论推导消除启发式调参，提出三种平衡策略，其中治疗聚合策略实现O(1)可扩展性，并扩展为生成架构Multi-Treatment CausalEGM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多治疗场景下的个体治疗效果估计面临两个关键挑战：平衡权重的超参数选择困境（需要昂贵的启发式调参）和计算可扩展性的维度诅咒（随着治疗空间扩展，计算复杂度急剧增加）。现有方法在这两方面存在显著不足。

Method: 1. 推导多治疗泛化边界并提出理论估计器来优化平衡权重α，消除启发式调参；2. 研究三种平衡策略：成对平衡、一对多平衡和治疗聚合；3. 扩展为生成架构Multi-Treatment CausalEGM，保留治疗流形的Wasserstein测地线结构。

Result: 在低维设置中，一对多平衡策略达到最优精度；治疗聚合策略在治疗空间扩展时同时保证精度和O(1)可扩展性。在半合成和图像数据集上的实验表明，该方法在估计精度和效率上显著优于传统模型，特别是在大规模干预场景中。

Conclusion: 本文提出的理论框架有效解决了多治疗ITE估计中的超参数选择和计算可扩展性问题，治疗聚合策略实现了精度与效率的平衡，生成架构Multi-Treatment CausalEGM进一步提升了性能，为大规模干预场景提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating Individual Treatment Effects (ITE) in multi-treatment scenarios faces two critical challenges: the Hyperparameter Selection Dilemma for balancing weights and the Curse of Dimensionality in computational scalability. This paper derives a novel multi-treatment generalization bound and proposes a theoretical estimator for the optimal balancing weight $α$, eliminating expensive heuristic tuning. We investigate three balancing strategies: Pairwise, One-vs-All (OVA), and Treatment Aggregation. While OVA achieves superior precision in low-dimensional settings, our proposed Treatment Aggregation ensures both accuracy and O(1) scalability as the treatment space expands. Furthermore, we extend our framework to a generative architecture, Multi-Treatment CausalEGM, which preserves the Wasserstein geodesic structure of the treatment manifold. Experiments on semi-synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms traditional models in estimation accuracy and efficiency, particularly in large-scale intervention scenarios.

</details>


### [93] [MobileKernelBench: Can LLMs Write Efficient Kernels for Mobile Devices?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11935)
*Xingze Zou,Jing Wang,Yuhua Zheng,Xueyi Chen,Haolei Bai,Lingcheng Kong,Syed A. R. Abu-Bakar,Zhaode Wang,Chengfei Lv,Haoji Hu,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs在移动设备内核生成方面存在挑战，作者提出MobileKernelBench评估框架和MoKA多智能体系统，显著提升编译成功率和性能优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLMs在移动设备内核生成领域的潜力，解决当前LLMs在移动框架工程复杂性和数据稀缺性方面的局限性

Method: 1) 引入MobileKernelBench评估框架，包含强调算子多样性和跨框架互操作性的基准测试，以及用于设备验证的自动化流水线；2) 提出Mobile Kernel Agent (MoKA)多智能体系统，具备仓库感知推理和计划-执行范式

Result: 1) 当前LLMs在MNN CPU后端上编译失败率超过54%，性能增益可忽略；2) MoKA将编译成功率提升至93.7%，27.4%生成的内核相比原生库实现可测量的加速

Conclusion: LLMs在移动设备内核生成方面面临显著挑战，但通过专门设计的评估框架和多智能体系统可以显著改善性能，为移动设备高效内核生成开辟了新途径

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in code generation, yet their potential for generating kernels specifically for mobile de- vices remains largely unexplored. In this work, we extend the scope of automated kernel generation to the mobile domain to investigate the central question: Can LLMs write efficient kernels for mobile devices? To enable systematic investigation, we introduce MobileKernelBench, a comprehensive evaluation framework comprising a benchmark prioritizing operator diversity and cross-framework interoperability, coupled with an automated pipeline that bridges the host-device gap for on-device verification. Leveraging this framework, we conduct extensive evaluation on the CPU backend of Mobile Neural Network (MNN), revealing that current LLMs struggle with the engineering complexity and data scarcity inher-ent to mobile frameworks; standard models and even fine-tuned variants exhibit high compilation failure rates (over 54%) and negligible performance gains due to hallucinations and a lack of domain-specific grounding. To overcome these limitations, we propose the Mobile K ernel A gent (MoKA), a multi-agent system equipped with repository-aware reasoning and a plan-and-execute paradigm.Validated on MobileKernelBench, MoKA achieves state-of-the-art performance, boosting compilation success to 93.7% and enabling 27.4% of generated kernelsto deliver measurable speedups over native libraries.

</details>


### [94] [Exhaustive Circuit Mapping of a Single-Cell Foundation Model Reveals Massive Redundancy, Heavy-Tailed Hub Architecture, and Layer-Dependent Differentiation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11940)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过三种实验方法改进了生物基础模型的机制可解释性：1）穷举电路追踪揭示了特征连接的重尾分布和注释偏差；2）高阶组合消融显示模型在所有测试阶数上都是次可加的；3）轨迹引导的特征操控建立了层位置与分化方向性之间的因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物基础模型的机制可解释性研究通常依赖于选择性特征采样、成对交互测试和观察性轨迹分析，这些方法都可能引入系统性偏差。本研究旨在通过更全面的方法克服这些局限性，从相关性证据转向因果证据。

Method: 研究在Geneformer（基于Transformer的单细胞基础模型）上进行了三个实验：1）对第5层所有4065个活跃稀疏自编码器特征进行穷举电路追踪；2）对8个特征三元组进行三阶组合消融；3）通过轨迹引导的特征操控来建立层位置与分化方向性之间的因果关系。

Result: 1）穷举追踪发现了1,393,850条显著下游边，比选择性采样扩展27倍，揭示了1.8%的特征占据不成比例的连接性，且40%的top20枢纽缺乏生物注释；2）三阶组合消融显示冗余度随交互阶数单调加深（三阶比0.59 vs 成对比0.74），无协同效应；3）轨迹操控显示第17层晚期特征始终推动细胞状态向成熟分化（阳性比例1.0），而第0层和第11层的早中期特征主要推动远离成熟（阳性比例0.00-0.58）。

Conclusion: 该研究通过穷举电路追踪、高阶组合消融和因果轨迹操控三种方法，从相关性证据转向了层依赖性细胞状态控制的因果证据，揭示了先前选择性分析中的系统性注释偏差，并证实了模型架构在所有测试阶数上的次可加性。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of biological foundation models has relied on selective feature sampling, pairwise interaction testing, and observational trajectory analysis. Each of these can introduce systematic bias. Here we present three experiments that address these limitations through exhaustive circuit tracing, higher order combinatorial ablation, and causal trajectory steering in Geneformer, a transformer based single cell foundation model. First, exhaustive tracing of all 4065 active sparse autoencoder features at layer 5 yields 1393850 significant downstream edges, a 27 fold expansion over selective sampling. This reveals a heavy tailed hub distribution in which 1.8 percent of features account for disproportionate connectivity and 40 percent of the top 20 hubs lack biological annotation. These results indicate systematic annotation bias in prior selective analyses. Second, three way combinatorial ablation across 8 feature triplets shows that redundancy deepens monotonically with interaction order, with a three way ratio of 0.59 versus a pairwise ratio of 0.74, and with zero synergy. This confirms that the model architecture is subadditive at all tested orders. Third, trajectory guided feature steering establishes a causal link between layer position and differentiation directionality. Late layer features at L17 consistently push cell states toward maturity, with fraction positive equal to 1.0. Early and mid layer features at L0 and L11 mostly push away from maturity, with fraction positive ranging from 0.00 to 0.58. Together these results move from correlation toward causal evidence for layer dependent control of cell state.

</details>


### [95] [Geometry-Aware Probabilistic Circuits via Voronoi Tessellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11946)
*Sahil Sidheekh,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将Voronoi剖分集成到概率电路中，以捕获数据流形的局部几何结构，同时保持可处理推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率电路使用数据无关的混合权重，限制了其捕获数据流形局部几何结构的能力，需要一种既能保持可处理推理又能融入几何结构的方法

Method: 提出两种互补解决方案：1) 近似推理框架，提供推理的保证上下界；2) 在Voronoi剖分下恢复精确可处理推理的结构条件；引入可微松弛实现梯度学习

Result: 在标准密度估计任务上经验验证了所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: Voronoi剖分是向概率电路融入几何结构的自然方式，通过适当的理论框架和可微松弛，既能保持推理可处理性，又能提升模型表达能力

Abstract: Probabilistic circuits (PCs) enable exact and tractable inference but employ data independent mixture weights that limit their ability to capture local geometry of the data manifold. We propose Voronoi tessellations (VT) as a natural way to incorporate geometric structure directly into the sum nodes of a PC. However, naïvely introducing such structure breaks tractability. We formalize this incompatibility and develop two complementary solutions: (1) an approximate inference framework that provides guaranteed lower and upper bounds for inference, and (2) a structural condition for VT under which exact tractable inference is recovered. Finally, we introduce a differentiable relaxation for VT that enables gradient-based learning and empirically validate the resulting approach on standard density estimation tasks.

</details>


### [96] [Flowcean - Model Learning for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12015)
*Maximilian Schmidt,Swantje Plambeck,Markus Knitt,Hendrik Rose,Goerschwin Fey,Jan Christian Wieck,Stephan Balduin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flowcean是一个用于自动化生成CPS数据驱动模型的模块化框架，通过集成多种学习策略、数据处理方法和评估指标，简化模型构建过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS模型构建困难且耗时，传统方法难以应对其固有复杂性，因此需要数据驱动的机器学习方法来简化模型生成过程。

Method: 开发Flowcean框架，采用模块化架构设计，集成多种学习策略、数据处理方法和评估指标，支持与不同学习库和工具的灵活集成。

Result: Flowcean提供了一个全面的解决方案，专门针对CPS场景定制，能够简化模型生成和评估过程，提高效率并增强可访问性。

Conclusion: Flowcean框架通过其模块化、灵活的设计，为CPS数据驱动模型生成提供了高效、可访问的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Effective models of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) are crucial for their design and operation. Constructing such models is difficult and time-consuming due to the inherent complexity of CPS. As a result, data-driven model generation using machine learning methods is gaining popularity. In this paper, we present Flowcean, a novel framework designed to automate the generation of models through data-driven learning that focuses on modularity and usability. By offering various learning strategies, data processing methods, and evaluation metrics, our framework provides a comprehensive solution, tailored to CPS scenarios. Flowcean facilitates the integration of diverse learning libraries and tools within a modular and flexible architecture, ensuring adaptability to a wide range of modeling tasks. This streamlines the process of model generation and evaluation, making it more efficient and accessible.

</details>


### [97] [Cornserve: A Distributed Serving System for Any-to-Any Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12118)
*Jae-Won Chung,Jeff J. Ma,Jisang Ahn,Yizhuo Liang,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cornserve是一个用于Any-to-Any多模态模型的分布式服务系统，通过组件解耦和独立扩展实现高效服务，相比现有系统提供最高3.81倍吞吐量和5.79倍更低尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Any-to-Any多模态模型的服务面临挑战：不同请求的输入输出模态组合不同，导致计算图路径不同，且模型各组件具有不同的扩展特性。现有系统难以灵活处理这种异构性。

Method: 1) 提供灵活的任务抽象来表达Any-to-Any模型计算图，支持组件解耦和独立扩展；2) 分布式运行时通过高效的记录-重放执行模型调度计算，跟踪数据依赖；3) 组件间直接转发张量数据，从生产者到消费者；4) 基于Kubernetes实现，约23K行Python代码。

Result: Cornserve支持多样化的Any-to-Any模型，相比现有系统提供最高3.81倍的吞吐量提升和5.79倍的尾部延迟降低。系统已开源，演示视频可在YouTube上获取。

Conclusion: Cornserve通过灵活的组件抽象和高效的分布式执行模型，成功解决了Any-to-Any多模态模型的服务挑战，实现了显著的性能提升，为这类新兴模型提供了实用的生产级服务解决方案。

Abstract: Any-to-Any models are an emerging class of multimodal models that accept combinations of multimodal data (e.g., text, image, video, audio) as input and generate them as output. Serving these models are challenging; different requests with different input and output modalities traverse different paths through the model computation graph, and each component of the model have different scaling characteristics.
  We present Cornserve, a distributed serving system for generic Any-to-Any models. Cornserve provides a flexible task abstraction for expressing Any-to-Any model computation graphs, enabling component disaggregation and independent scaling. The distributed runtime dispatches compute to the data plane via an efficient record-and-replay execution model that keeps track of data dependencies, and forwards tensor data between components directly from the producer to the consumer. Built on Kubernetes with approximately 23K new lines of Python, Cornserve supports diverse Any-to-Any models and delivers up to 3.81$\times$ higher throughput and 5.79$\times$ lower tail latency. Cornserve is open-source, and the demo video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [98] [IsoCompute Playbook: Optimally Scaling Sampling Compute for LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12151)
*Zhoujun Cheng,Yutao Xie,Yuxiao Qu,Amrith Setlur,Shibo Hao,Varad Pimpalkhute,Tongtong Liang,Feng Yao,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Virginia Smith,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Zhiting Hu,Taylor Killian,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大规模语言模型（LLMs）强化学习（RL）后训练的计算最优分配问题，提出了基于三个资源维度（每个问题的并行rollout数量、每批问题数量、更新步数）的缩放定律，发现并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测地增长然后饱和，并揭示了不同难度问题的不同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然缩放定律指导了LLM预训练的计算分配，但针对大规模语言模型强化学习后训练的类似指导原则仍然缺乏理解。当前缺乏关于如何在RL后训练中优化计算资源分配的明确指导。

Method: 将RL缩放问题构建为计算约束下的优化问题，研究三个关键资源维度：每个问题的并行rollout数量、每批问题数量、更新步数。通过实验分析这些维度在不同计算预算下的最优分配，并探索不同难度问题（简单和困难）的机制差异。

Result: 发现计算最优的并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测地增长然后饱和。简单问题和困难问题表现出不同机制：简单问题通过解决方案锐化驱动，困难问题通过覆盖扩展驱动。增加并行rollout数量能减轻问题间的干扰，而每批问题数量主要影响训练稳定性且可在较宽范围内选择。

Conclusion: 研究结果为LLM RL后训练提供了实用的计算效率指导，将RL缩放定律重新构建为规范性的分配规则，并在不同基础模型和数据分布上得到验证，为高效RL后训练提供了可操作的指导原则。

Abstract: While scaling laws guide compute allocation for LLM pre-training, analogous prescriptions for reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) remain poorly understood. We study the compute-optimal allocation of sampling compute for on-policy RL methods in LLMs, framing scaling as a compute-constrained optimization over three resources: parallel rollouts per problem, number of problems per batch, and number of update steps. We find that the compute-optimal number of parallel rollouts per problem increases predictably with compute budget and then saturates. This trend holds across both easy and hard problems, though driven by different mechanisms: solution sharpening on easy problems and coverage expansion on hard problems. We further show that increasing the number of parallel rollouts mitigates interference across problems, while the number of problems per batch primarily affects training stability and can be chosen within a broad range. Validated across base models and data distributions, our results recast RL scaling laws as prescriptive allocation rules and provide practical guidance for compute-efficient LLM RL post-training.

</details>


### [99] [A Quantitative Characterization of Forgetting in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12163)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论角度分析了生成模型持续后训练中的遗忘现象，提出了两种遗忘形式（质量遗忘和旧组件漂移），并证明了不同KL散度目标对遗忘的不同影响，还分析了回放机制和三种近期方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的持续后训练被广泛使用，但对于何时以及为何发生遗忘现象缺乏原则性的理解。本文旨在从理论角度系统分析遗忘现象，为理解遗忘机制提供理论基础。

Method: 采用Chen等人提出的双模态混合抽象模型（代表旧任务和新任务），在等协方差高斯模态假设下，理论分析前向KL和后向KL目标对遗忘的影响。分析回放机制与这些目标的交互作用，并通过相同理论框架分析三种近期提出的近策略后训练方法（SDFT、TTT-Discover、OAPL）。

Result: 证明前向KL目标训练新分布数据会导致旧权重归零（质量遗忘），而后向KL目标收敛到真实目标（避免质量遗忘），旧均值漂移受Bhattacharyya系数控制的重叠门控误分配概率影响，漂移随模态分离呈指数衰减。回放机制对前向KL需要修改训练分布，对后向KL则通过有界重要性加权防止有限批次旧模态饥饿。三种近期方法在特定条件下能保留旧质量并表现出重叠控制的漂移。

Conclusion: 遗忘现象可以基于散度方向、几何行为重叠、采样机制以及训练中过去行为的可见性之间的相互作用进行精确量化。该理论框架为理解和设计抗遗忘的持续学习算法提供了基础。

Abstract: Continual post-training of generative models is widely used, yet a principled understanding of when and why forgetting occurs remains limited. We develop theoretical results under a two-mode mixture abstraction (representing old and new tasks), proposed by Chen et al. (2025) (arXiv:2510.18874), and formalize forgetting in two forms: (i) mass forgetting, where the old mixture weight collapses to zero, and (ii) old-component drift, where an already-correct old component shifts during training. For equal-covariance Gaussian modes, we prove that forward-KL objectives trained on data from the new distribution drive the old weight to zero, while reverse-KL objectives converge to the true target (thereby avoiding mass forgetting) and perturb the old mean only through overlap-gated misassignment probabilities controlled by the Bhattacharyya coefficient, yielding drift that decays exponentially with mode separation and a locally well-conditioned geometry with exponential convergence. We further quantify how replay interacts with these objectives. For forward-KL, replay must modify the training distribution to change the population optimum; for reverse-KL, replay leaves the population objective unchanged but prevents finite-batch old-mode starvation through bounded importance weighting. Finally, we analyze three recently proposed near-on-policy post-training methods, SDFT (arxiv:2601.19897), TTT-Discover (arxiv:2601.16175), and OAPL (arxiv:2602.19362), via the same lens and derive explicit conditions under which each retains old mass and exhibits overlap-controlled drift. Overall, our results show that forgetting can by precisely quantified based on the interaction between divergence direction, geometric behavioral overlap, sampling regime, and the visibility of past behavior during training.

</details>


### [100] [Neural Thickets: Diverse Task Experts Are Dense Around Pretrained Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12228)
*Yulu Gan,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将预训练结果视为参数向量分布而非单一向量，发现大模型中任务专家密度显著增加，并基于此开发了一种简单的并行后训练方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将预训练结果视为单一参数向量作为后续优化的起点，但作者认为预训练实际上产生的是参数向量分布，其中已包含任务特定的专家解

Method: 提出完全并行的后训练方法：随机采样N个参数扰动，选择表现最好的K个，通过多数投票集成预测结果

Result: 该方法在当代大规模模型中与PPO、GRPO、ES等标准后训练方法具有竞争力，尽管方法简单但效果显著

Conclusion: 大模型预训练产生的参数分布中任务专家密度显著增加，使得简单的随机采样和集成方法能够有效发现专家解，这为后训练提供了新视角

Abstract: Pretraining produces a learned parameter vector that is typically treated as a starting point for further iterative adaptation. In this work, we instead view the outcome of pretraining as a distribution over parameter vectors, whose support already contains task-specific experts. We show that in small models such expert solutions occupy a negligible fraction of the volume of this distribution, making their discovery reliant on structured optimization methods such as gradient descent. In contrast, in large, well-pretrained models the density of task-experts increases dramatically, so that diverse, task-improving specialists populate a substantial fraction of the neighborhood around the pretrained weights. Motivated by this perspective, we explore a simple, fully parallel post-training method that samples $N$ parameter perturbations at random, selects the top $K$, and ensembles predictions via majority vote. Despite its simplicity, this approach is competitive with standard post-training methods such as PPO, GRPO, and ES for contemporary large-scale models.

</details>


### [101] [Temporal Straightening for Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12231)
*Ying Wang,Oumayma Bounou,Gaoyue Zhou,Randall Balestriero,Tim G. J. Rudner,Yann LeCun,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间平直化方法，通过曲率正则化改进世界模型中的表示学习，使潜在空间中的欧氏距离更好地近似测地距离，提升梯度规划效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉编码器产生的特征虽然语义丰富，但并非专门为规划任务设计，包含对规划无关甚至有害的信息。受人类视觉处理中感知平直化假设启发，需要改进表示学习以更好地支持潜在规划

Method: 引入时间平直化方法，使用曲率正则化器鼓励局部平直的潜在轨迹，联合学习编码器和预测器。该方法减少潜在轨迹的曲率，使潜在空间中的欧氏距离更好地近似测地距离，并改善规划目标的调节性

Result: 实验证明时间平直化使基于梯度的规划更加稳定，在一系列目标达成任务中显著提高了成功率

Conclusion: 时间平直化通过曲率正则化改进表示学习，使潜在空间几何更适合规划任务，提高了基于世界模型的潜在规划性能

Abstract: Learning good representations is essential for latent planning with world models. While pretrained visual encoders produce strong semantic visual features, they are not tailored to planning and contain information irrelevant -- or even detrimental -- to planning. Inspired by the perceptual straightening hypothesis in human visual processing, we introduce temporal straightening to improve representation learning for latent planning. Using a curvature regularizer that encourages locally straightened latent trajectories, we jointly learn an encoder and a predictor. We show that reducing curvature this way makes the Euclidean distance in latent space a better proxy for the geodesic distance and improves the conditioning of the planning objective. We demonstrate empirically that temporal straightening makes gradient-based planning more stable and yields significantly higher success rates across a suite of goal-reaching tasks.

</details>


### [102] [STAMP: Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12237)
*Fengwei Tian,Payel Bhattacharjee,Heidi Hanson,Geoffrey D. Rubin,Joseph Y. Lo,Ravi Tandon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP框架通过选择性分配隐私预算和极坐标机制实现任务感知的文本隐私保护，在隐私保护与下游任务效用间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本隐私保护方法通常对所有token应用相同的噪声水平，忽视了不同token对下游任务的重要性差异及其隐私敏感性的不同，导致隐私保护与任务效用之间的权衡不够优化。

Method: STAMP框架包含两个核心组件：1）选择性隐私预算分配机制，基于token对下游任务的重要性（通过任务特定表示衡量）和隐私敏感性（如姓名、日期、标识符）动态分配隐私预算；2）极坐标机制，在单位球面上仅扰动嵌入向量的方向而保持其幅度不变，解码时使用余弦最近邻搜索。

Result: 在SQuAD、Yelp和AG News数据集上的实验表明，STAMP结合归一化极坐标机制在不同token隐私预算下均能实现优越的隐私-效用权衡，相比各向同性噪声机制更好地保持了嵌入空间的语义邻域。

Conclusion: STAMP通过细粒度、组级别的隐私预算控制和几何对齐的扰动机制，为任务感知的文本隐私保护提供了有效框架，显著改善了隐私保护与下游任务效用之间的平衡。

Abstract: We present STAMP (Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy), a new framework for task-aware text privatization that achieves an improved privacy-utility trade-off. STAMP selectively allocates privacy budgets across tokens by jointly considering (i) each token's importance to the downstream task (as measured via a task- or query-specific representation), and (ii) its privacy sensitivity (e.g., names, dates, identifiers). This token-level partitioning enables fine-grained, group-wise control over the level of noise applied to different parts of the input, balancing privacy protection with task relevance. To privatize individual token embeddings, we introduce the polar mechanism, which perturbs only the direction of embeddings on the unit sphere while preserving their magnitude. Decoding is performed via cosine nearest-neighbor search, aligning the perturbation geometry with the decoding geometry. Unlike isotropic noise mechanisms, the polar mechanism maintains semantic neighborhoods in the embedding space and better preserves downstream utility. Experimental evaluations on SQuAD, Yelp, and AG News datasets demonstrate that STAMP, when combined with the normalized polar mechanism, consistently achieves superior privacy-utility trade-offs across varying per-token privacy budgets.

</details>


### [103] [The Latent Color Subspace: Emergent Order in High-Dimensional Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12261)
*Mateusz Pach,Jessica Bader,Quentin Bouniot,Serge Belongie,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种解释FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中颜色表示的方法，揭示其反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构，并基于此开发无需训练的颜色控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型发展迅速，但实现细粒度控制仍然困难，主要由于对语义信息编码方式的理解有限。需要更好地理解潜在空间中颜色信息的表示方式。

Method: 开发了对FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中颜色表示的解释方法，揭示了反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构。基于此提出了潜在颜色子空间(LCS)解释，并引入了一种完全无需训练的基于闭式潜在空间操作的颜色控制方法。

Result: 验证了LCS解释的有效性，证明其既能预测颜色，又能显式控制颜色。该方法在FLUX中实现了无需训练的颜色控制，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 揭示了FLUX.1潜在空间中的颜色表示结构，为细粒度图像控制提供了新的理解和方法，有助于推动文本到图像生成模型的可控性发展。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models have advanced rapidly, yet achieving fine-grained control over generated images remains difficult, largely due to limited understanding of how semantic information is encoded. We develop an interpretation of the color representation in the Variational Autoencoder latent space of FLUX.1 [Dev], revealing a structure reflecting Hue, Saturation, and Lightness. We verify our Latent Color Subspace (LCS) interpretation by demonstrating that it can both predict and explicitly control color, introducing a fully training-free method in FLUX based solely on closed-form latent-space manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [First and second-order optimality conditions for a bilinear controlled wave equation on an infinite horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11271)
*Redouane El Mezegueldy,Zakarya Dardour*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无限时间域上双线性阻尼波动方程的最优控制问题，建立了控制系统的适定性、最优控制的存在性，并推导了一阶和二阶最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限时间域上双线性阻尼波动方程的最优控制问题，旨在为这类双线性双曲控制系统提供完整的局部最优性特征描述。

Method: 建立控制系统的适定性和一致能量估计；通过构造极小化序列证明最优控制的存在性；证明控制到状态映射的二阶连续Fréchet可微性；推导一阶必要条件（变分不等式和点投影公式）以及二阶必要和充分条件（Hessian的非负性和强制性）。

Result: 证明了控制系统的适定性、最优控制的存在性；控制到状态映射的二阶连续Fréchet可微性；推导了一阶最优性条件（变分不等式和点投影公式）；建立了二阶必要条件（Hessian非负性）和充分条件（Hessian强制性），完整刻画了局部最优性。

Conclusion: 该研究为有界空间域上无限时间域的双线性双曲控制系统提供了完整的局部最优性特征描述，包括适定性、最优控制存在性、一阶和二阶最优性条件，为这类系统的优化控制奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates the optimal control of a bilinear damped wave equation over an infinite time horizon. We establish the well-posedness of the controlled system and derive uniform energy estimates. The existence of optimal controls is proven by constructing a minimizing sequence. We prove that the control-to-state mapping is twice continuously Fréchet differentiable, which enables the derivation of first-order necessary optimality conditions in the form of a variational inequality and a pointwise projection formula. Furthermore, we establish second-order necessary and sufficient conditions: the nonnegativity of the Hessian of the cost functional is shown to be a necessary condition for local optimality, while the coercivity of this Hessian constitutes a sufficient condition. These results provide a complete characterization of local optimality for bilinear hyperbolic control systems over infinite time horizons on bounded spatial domains.

</details>


### [105] [Minimizers that are not Impulsive Minimizers and Higher Order Abnormality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11302)
*Monica Motta,Michele Palladino,Franco Rampazzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了最优控制中的两个相关问题：1) 分离集方法与惩罚技术之间的兼容性问题，通过QDQ逼近锥与Clarke切锥的等价性分析；2) 将兼容性结果应用于无界控制和脉冲扩展问题中的下确界间隙现象，建立了严格意义极小化器的异常性与高阶极大值原理的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机源于最优控制理论中两个经典方法（分离集方法和惩罚技术）在推导具有最终目标的最优控制问题必要条件时的不兼容性。这种不兼容性主要源于两种方法对目标处切性概念的不同定义。此外，作者旨在将兼容性分析应用于研究无界控制和脉冲扩展问题中的下确界间隙现象。

Method: 方法分为两部分：第一部分引入拟微分商（QDQ）逼近锥（适用于分离集方法），并确定Clarke切锥（惩罚技术的典型工具）也是QDQ逼近锥的条件，特别是在局部不变性假设或目标局部与r-邻近正则集重合的情况下。第二部分将兼容性结果应用于分析无界控制和脉冲扩展问题中的下确界间隙，通过拓扑论证和兼容性分析，将异常性-间隙对应关系从扩展意义极小化器推广到严格意义极小化器。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 在适当的局部不变性假设下，或当目标局部与r-邻近正则集重合时，Clarke切锥也是QDQ逼近锥，从而解决了两种方法的兼容性问题；2) 建立了严格意义极小化器的下确界间隙发生与涉及李括号的高阶极大值原理中异常性之间的对应关系，这一结果通过拓扑论证和兼容性分析得以实现。

Conclusion: 本文通过分析QDQ逼近锥与Clarke切锥的等价条件，解决了最优控制中分离集方法与惩罚技术之间的兼容性问题。进一步地，利用这一兼容性结果，成功将异常性-下确界间隙对应关系从扩展意义（脉冲）极小化器推广到严格意义极小化器，为无界控制和脉冲扩展问题的高阶最优性条件提供了理论支持。

Abstract: This paper addresses two related problems in optimal control. The first investigation consists of compatibility issues between two classical approaches to deriving necessary conditions for optimal control problems with a final target: the set-separation approach and penalization techniques. These methods generally lead to non-equivalent conditions, mainly due to their reliance on different notions of tangency at the target.
  We address this issue by considering Quasi Differential Quotient (QDQ) approximating cones (which are fit for the set-separation approach) and identifying conditions under which the Clarke tangent cone (which is a typical tool within penalization techniques) is also a QDQ approximating cone. In particular, we show that this property holds under suitable local invariance assumptions or when the target coincides locally with an $r$-prox regular set.
  In the second part of the paper we apply this compatibility result to the study of infimum-gap phenomena in optimal control problems with unbounded controls and impulsive extensions. In particular, we establish a connection between the occurrence of infimum gaps for strict-sense minimizers and abnormality in a higher-order Maximum Principle involving Lie brackets. While the abnormality-gap correspondence beyond first-order conditions has been already established for extended-sense --i.e. impulsive-- minimizers, a topological argument involving the former and the utilization of the above compatibility issues allow us to extend this correspondence to strict-sense minimizers.

</details>


### [106] [Blind Hyperspectral and Multispectral Images Fusion: A Unified Tensor Fusion Framework from Coupled Inverse Problem Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11530)
*Ying Gao,Michael K. Ng,Chunfeng cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于高光谱和多光谱图像盲融合的统一张量框架，能够同时估计目标HR-HSI、空间点扩散函数和光谱响应函数，无需预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高光谱和多光谱图像融合方法通常假设空间模糊算子和光谱响应算子是已知的，但这种假设在实际应用中限制性很强。为了解决这一局限性，需要开发能够同时估计未知算子的盲融合方法。

Method: 将盲融合问题建模为耦合逆问题，结合空间域的盲去卷积和光谱域的盲解混。提出了一个统一的张量融合框架，采用部分线性化的ADMM算法配合Moreau包络平滑进行优化，并设计了针对融合问题特性的初始化估计器。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上与最先进方法进行数值比较，证明了所提方法具有出色的性能，能够实现灵活的自调整和实时融合。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个有效的盲高光谱-多光谱图像融合框架，能够同时估计未知的空间和光谱算子，克服了传统方法对先验知识的依赖，在实际应用中具有更好的适应性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral and multispectral images fusion aims at integrating a low-resolution hyperspectral image (LR-HSI) and a high-resolution multispectral image (HR-MSI) to construct a high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI). It is generally assumed that spatial blurring operator and spectral response operator are prior-known. However, such an assumption is extremely restrictive in practice. To overcome this limitation, this paper formulates blind fusion as a coupled inverse problem, integrating blind deconvolution in the spatial domain with blind unmixing in the spectral domain. From this novel perspective, we propose a unified tensor fusion framework capable of flexible self-adjustment and real-time fusion without pre-training. We further introduce an optimization model for the joint estimation of the target HR-HSI, the spatial point spread function, and the spectral response function. To solve this model, we devise a partially linearized alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm with Moreau envelope smoothing, accompanied by the rigorous convergence analysis. An initialization estimator tailored to the specific characteristics of the fusion problem is proposed. Numerical comparisons with state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the compelling performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [107] [Simultaneous estimation of multiple discrete unimodal distributions under stochastic order constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11532)
*Yasuhiro Yoshida,Noriyoshi Sukegawa,Jiro Iwanaga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于随机序约束的多离散单峰分布估计方法，通过混合整数凸二次优化求解，在小样本情况下显著提升估计精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受现实世界平台搜索行为分析驱动，需要在先验知识（分布间的优先关系）约束下估计多个离散单峰分布

Method: 将随机序约束纳入估计问题，构建混合整数凸二次优化模型进行求解

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，小样本时Jensen-Shannon散度平均降低2.2%（最高6.3%），数据充足时与现有方法性能相当

Conclusion: 提出的随机序约束方法能有效利用先验知识，在小样本情况下显著改善多离散单峰分布的估计精度

Abstract: We study the problem of estimating multiple discrete unimodal distributions, motivated by search behavior analysis on a real-world platform. To incorporate prior knowledge of precedence relations among distributions, we impose stochastic order constraints and formulate the estimation task as a mixed-integer convex quadratic optimization problem. Experiments on both synthetic and real datasets show that the proposed method reduces the Jensen-Shannon divergence by 2.2% on average (up to 6.3%) when the sample size is small, while performing comparably to existing methods when sufficient data are available.

</details>


### [108] [Families of Two-Impulse Optimal Rendezvous Transfers Between Elliptic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11538)
*Beom Park,Kathleen C. Howell,Jaewoo Kim,Jaemyung Ahn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文从族系角度重新审视了开普勒轨道间的经典燃料最优双脉冲交会问题，揭示了看似孤立的优化解实际上是连续解族的成员，并通过数值延拓方法追踪这些解族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常产生孤立的优化解，这些解之间的相互关系不明确。作者希望从族系角度揭示这些优化解之间的内在联系，提供对解空间的全局理解。

Method: 提出一个框架，强制执行一阶必要优化条件的子集，通过数值延拓追踪由此产生的一参数解族。使用基于Hessian的准则和Primer Vector理论对解族进行分类，并将解族投影到porkchop图上以连接角度和时间域。

Result: 代表性案例研究表明，在轨道几何变化下，局部优化分支会出现、合并和消失，提供了解空间的全局映射。这种互补视角阐明了优化解的鲁棒性，并识别了名义轨迹附近的替代近优化转移。

Conclusion: 从族系角度分析燃料最优双脉冲交会问题，揭示了优化解之间的连续连接关系，提供了对解空间结构的全局理解，有助于识别鲁棒性优化解和替代近优化转移方案。

Abstract: The classical fuel-optimal two-impulse rendezvous problem between Keplerian orbits is revisited from a family-based perspective. Conventional approaches often yield isolated optimal solutions whose mutual relationships remain unclear; yet, when re-parameterized appropriately, seemingly unrelated optima are revealed to be connected members of continuous solution families. To expose this structure, the proposed framework enforces a subset of first-order necessary optimality conditions and traces the resulting one-parameter families via numerical continuation. The families are classified using Hessian-based criteria and Primer Vector Theory, and are projected onto porkchop plots to connect the angular and temporal domains. Representative case studies reveal the emergence, merging, and disappearance of locally optimal branches under variations in orbital geometry, supplying a global map of the solution landscape. This complementary perspective clarifies the robustness of optimal solutions and identifies alternative near-optimal transfers in the vicinity of a nominal trajectory.

</details>


### [109] [Exploiting Parallelism in a QPALM-based Solver for Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11723)
*Pieter Pas,Kristoffer Fink Løwenstein,Daniele Bernardini,Panagiotis Patrinos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: QPALM-OCP算法针对最优控制中的二次规划问题，通过并行化和向量化技术优化C++实现，相比原始QPALM方法在计算效率上有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优控制问题中产生的二次规划问题具有特定的结构特点，其中不同阶段的计算工作可以独立进行，这为并行化提供了机会。作者旨在利用这种结构特点来提升QPALM-OCP算法的计算效率。

Method: 针对最优控制问题中不同阶段计算可独立进行的特点，采用并行化和向量化技术，开发了优化的C++实现。该方法充分利用了最优控制问题的特定结构，将计算任务分配到多个处理单元上同时执行。

Result: 在最优控制基准问题上进行了测试，并与原始QPALM方法进行了比较。结果显示，通过并行化和向量化技术，优化后的实现显著提升了计算性能。

Conclusion: 最优控制问题中二次规划的特殊结构为并行化提供了良好机会，通过针对性的并行化和向量化技术，可以显著提升QPALM-OCP算法的计算效率，使其在处理大规模最优控制问题时更具优势。

Abstract: We discuss the opportunities for parallelization in the recently proposed QPALM-OCP algorithm, a solver tailored to quadratic programs arising in optimal control. A significant part of the computational work can be carried out independently for the different stages in the optimal control problem. We exploit this specific structure to apply parallelization and vectorization techniques in an optimized C++ implementation of the method. Results for optimal control benchmark problems and comparisons to the original QPALM method are provided.

</details>


### [110] [An Eikonal Approach for Globally Optimal Free Flight Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11830)
*Ralf Borndörfer,Arturas Jocas,Martin Weiser*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于程函方程的方法，在静止风场中为飞机寻找连续全局最优轨迹以最小化排放和燃料消耗，通过构建信任区域解决数值离散误差导致的局部最优问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在静止风场中为飞机寻找全局最优轨迹以最小化排放和燃料消耗，但数值离散误差可能导致选择局部最优轨迹而非全局最优轨迹，特别是在目的地接近哈密顿-雅可比-贝尔曼方程的割迹时

Method: 基于程函方程的方法，利用有限元误差估计在割迹周围构建信任区域，确保在距离割迹足够远的目的地轨迹的唯一性

Result: 该方法能够找到连续全局最优轨迹，通过信任区域机制保证轨迹的唯一性，避免因数值离散误差而选择局部最优轨迹

Conclusion: 提出的基于程函方程的方法结合信任区域机制，能够有效解决飞机在静止风场中的全局轨迹优化问题，确保找到真正的最优轨迹并避免数值误差导致的次优选择

Abstract: We present an eikonal-based approach that is capable of finding a continuous globally optimal trajectory for an aircraft in a stationary wind field. This minimizes emissions and fuel consumption. If the destination is close to a cut locus of the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, small numerical discretization errors can lead to selecting a merely locally optimal trajectory and missing the globally optimal one. Based on finite element error estimates, we construct a trust region around the cut loci in order to guarantee uniqueness of trajectories for destinations sufficiently far from cut loci.

</details>


### [111] [Generalisation of Farkas' lemma beyond closedness: a constructive approach via Fenchel-Rockafellar duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11859)
*Camille Pouchol,Emmanuel Trélat,Christophe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于优化对偶理论和Fenchel-Rockafellar对偶的Farkas引理新推广方法，适用于由闭有界凸集生成的锥，不要求A(P)闭或P闭，并给出了b∈A(P)和b∈A(P)闭包的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Farkas引理要求A(P)闭，但这个条件难以验证且不总是成立。现有推广大多仍要求A(P)闭或至少P闭，限制了应用范围。需要开发更弱的假设条件来推广Farkas引理。

Method: 基于原始-对偶优化问题对和Fenchel-Rockafellar对偶理论，仅假设P由闭有界凸集生成。通过构造性特征刻画满足‖Ax-b‖≤ε的x∈P，利用对偶问题的最优性条件。

Result: 在P由闭有界凸集生成的弱假设下，获得了b∈A(P)和b∈A(P)闭包的充要条件。当ε=0时，特征强烈依赖于对偶问题是否有解，并讨论了确保解存在的条件。方法可松弛应用于非凸锥。

Conclusion: 该方法显著扩展了Farkas引理的应用范围，仅需P由闭有界凸集生成这一弱条件，不要求A(P)闭或P闭。提供了构造性特征和最优性条件，并能通过松弛处理非凸锥问题。

Abstract: Farkas' lemma is an ubiquitous tool in optimisation, as it provides necessary and sufficient conditions to have $b \in A(P)$, where $P$ is a closed convex cone, $A$ is a (continuous) linear mapping and $b$ is a fixed vector. The standard underlying hypothesis is the closedness of $A(P)$, which is not always satisfied and can be difficult to check. We devise a new method to generalise Farkas' lemma, based on a primal-dual pair of optimisation problems and Fenchel-Rockafellar duality theory. We work under the sole hypothesis that $P$ be generated by a closed bounded convex set. This hypothesis is weaker than in previous generalisations of Farkas' lemma, which almost all require that $A(P)$ be closed, or, in few cases, that only $P$ be closed. In our case, $P$ (and a fortiori $A(P)$) is not necessarily closed; we uncover necessary and sufficient conditions both for $b \in A(P)$ and $b \in \overline{A(P)}$. For a given $\e \geq 0$, we exhibit constructive characterisations of $x \in P$ such that $\|Ax-b\| \leq \e$ when it exists, by means of optimality conditions. For $\e = 0$, these strongly rely on whether the dual problem admits a solution, and we discuss conditions under which it does. Finally, we also explain how, upon relaxation, we may apply our method to a nonconvex cone.

</details>


### [112] [Asymptotically Efficient Recursive Identification Under One-Bit Communications Achieving Original CRLB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11964)
*Xingrui Liu,Jieming Ke,Mingjie Shao,Yanlong Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于一比特通信下自回归外生输入系统的渐近高效递归辨识算法，能渐近达到基于原始未量化数据的CRLB，相比现有方法降低至少36%的渐近均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一比特通信系统辨识方法通常只量化当前系统输出，导致在量化过程中产生不可忽略的信息损失，只能达到基于量化观测的CRLB，无法达到基于原始数据的CRLB。

Method: 提出一种新颖的量化方法，同时整合当前和历史系统输出与输入，在一比特数据中提供更丰富的参数信息；基于此技术设计相应的远程估计算法；建立新框架分析量化前组合数据的尾概率，避免传统对量化数据独立性的假设。

Result: 证明远程估计达到渐近正态性，误差协方差矩阵收敛到原始CRLB，确认其渐近高效性；相比现有一比特数据辨识算法，渐近均方误差降低至少36%；数值模拟验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合历史信息的新量化方法和相应的分析框架，成功实现了一比特通信下自回归外生输入系统的渐近高效辨识，突破了现有方法的信息损失限制。

Abstract: This paper develops an asymptotically efficient recursive identification algorithm for autoregressive systems with exogenous inputs under one-bit communications. In particular, the proposed method asymptotically achieves the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) based on the original data before quantization (original CRLB), whereas existing approaches typically attain only the CRLB corresponding to the quantized observations. The primary reason is that the existing methods quantize only the current system output, resulting in non-negligible information loss under one-bit quantization. To overcome this challenge, we present a novel quantization method that integrates both current and historical system outputs and inputs to provide richer parameter information in one-bit data, allowing the information loss caused by quantization to become a minor term relative to the original CRLB. Based on this technique, a corresponding remote estimation algorithm is further proposed. To address the convergence analysis challenge posed by the non-independence of the one-bit data, we establish a new framework that analyzes the tail probability of integrated data formed by combining current and historical system outputs and inputs before quantization, thereby eliminating the need for the traditional independence assumption on the quantized data. It is proven that the remote estimate achieves asymptotic normality, and the error covariance matrix converges to the original CRLB, confirming its asymptotic efficiency. Compared to existing identification algorithms under one-bit data, this method reduces the asymptotic mean squared error by at least 36%. Several numerical examples are simulated to show the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [113] [Gradient-robustness in optimization subject to stationary Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12028)
*Constanze Neutsch,Winnifried Wollner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 讨论非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题模拟及其最优控制的梯度鲁棒离散化方法，比较不同连续等价公式的离散化效果及其对伴随方程的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程模拟及其最优控制问题的数值离散方法，特别关注梯度鲁棒性（gradient robustness）这一重要数值特性，探讨不同数学公式对离散化效果和伴随方程计算的影响

Method: 分析非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题的多种连续等价公式，比较这些公式的梯度鲁棒离散化方法，研究不同公式对最优控制问题中伴随方程的影响

Result: 不同连续等价公式的梯度鲁棒离散化效果存在差异，选择的公式会显著影响最优控制问题中梯度计算所需的伴随方程

Conclusion: 非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题的最优控制数值模拟中，数学公式的选择对梯度鲁棒离散化和伴随方程计算具有重要影响，需要根据具体应用需求选择合适的公式

Abstract: In this article, we discuss gradient robust discretizations for the simulation of non-linear incompressible Navier-Stokes problem and the optimal control of such flow. We consider several formulations of the flow problem that are equivalent for the continuous non-linear forward problem and compare their gradient robust discretization. We will then discuss the influence of the chosen formulation on the adjoint equations needed for gradient computation in the solution of the optimal control problem.

</details>


### [114] [Well-posedness of boundary control systems and application to ISS for coupled heat equations with boundary disturbances and delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12029)
*Yassine El Gantouh,Jun Zheng,Guchuan Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究边界控制系统解的存在性和适定性，提供保证解对初始数据和L^p输入连续依赖的显式可验证条件，应用于边界耦合热方程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究边界控制系统的适定性问题，特别是解对初始数据和输入的连续依赖性，这对于实际工程应用中的稳定性分析至关重要

Method: 提出线性时不变无限维系统输入/输出映射的有界性估计新方法，应用于具有无界控制和观测算子的系统

Result: 建立了保证解连续依赖性的显式可验证条件，并应用于边界耦合热方程，推导出指数输入到状态稳定的具体条件

Conclusion: 该研究为边界控制系统提供了实用的适定性分析框架，特别适用于具有边界扰动和时滞的热方程系统

Abstract: This paper studies the existence of solutions and, in particular, the well-posedness of a class of boundary control systems. Our main result provides explicit and verifiable conditions on the system data that guarantee continuous dependence of solutions on the initial data and $L^p$-inputs. The proof relies on a new boundedness estimate for the input/output maps of linear time-invariant infinite-dimensional systems with unbounded control and observation operators. The developed technique is applied to derive specific conditions for the exponential input-to-state stability of boundary-coupled heat equations with boundary disturbances and time-delays.

</details>


### [115] [Controlled Swarm Gradient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12039)
*Louison Aubert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将受控模拟退火框架扩展到群体梯度动力学，通过构造速度场使过程边际分布精确跟踪退火曲线，实现任意速率的全局优化收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受控模拟退火方法在非凸势函数全局优化中存在局限性，需要扩展到更广泛的Langevin型平均场扩散过程，特别是噪声强度依赖于过程边际密度的群体梯度动力学。

Method: 首先分析群体梯度动力学的稳态概率密度，证明其在逆温度参数趋于无穷时收敛到全局最小值的支撑集；然后对任意非降冷却调度，构造求解连续性方程的速度场，叠加到群体梯度动力学上得到受控过程。

Result: 证明了受控群体动力学收敛到全局最小值，收敛速率原则上可由冷却调度任意控制；给出了受控动力学的算法实现，并与受控模拟退火进行了性能比较。

Conclusion: 成功将受控模拟退火框架扩展到群体梯度动力学，实现了任意速率的全局优化收敛，但数值实现存在一定局限性。

Abstract: We consider the global optimization of a non-convex potential $U : \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}$ and extend the controlled simulated annealing framework introduced by Molin et al. (2026) to the class of swarm gradient dynamics, a family of Langevin-type mean-field diffusions whose noise intensity depends locally on the marginal density of the process. Building on the time-homogeneous model of Huang and Malik (2025), we first analyze its invariant probability density and show that, as the inverse temperature parameter tends to infinity, it converges weakly to a probability measure supported on the set of global minimizers of $U$. This result justifies using this family of invariant measures as an annealing curve in a controlled swarm setting. Given an arbitrary non-decreasing cooling schedule, we then prove the existence of a velocity field solving the continuity equation associated with the curve of invariant densities. Superimposing this field onto the swarm gradient dynamics yields a well-posed controlled process whose marginal law follows exactly the prescribed annealing curve. As a consequence, the controlled swarm dynamics converges toward global minimizers with, in principle, arbitrarily fast convergence rates, entirely dictated by the choice of the cooling schedule. Finally, we discuss an algorithmic implementation of the controlled dynamics and compare its performance with controlled simulated annealing, highlighting some numerical limitations.

</details>


### [116] [Expanding Flow Shop Tasks Based on Recursive Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12077)
*Boris Kupriyanov,Alexander Lazarev,Alexandr Roschin,Frank Werner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文讨论了流水车间调度问题的递归表示法的多个扩展，展示了递归函数如何统一描述多种扩展问题，并提出了递归函数的一般要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水车间调度问题存在多种扩展形式，传统方法需要为每个扩展单独建模。本文旨在开发一种统一的递归表示框架，能够简洁地描述多种扩展问题，提高问题建模的通用性和灵活性。

Method: 提出了三种类型的递归函数：与机器相关的函数、基于约束调整处理时间的函数、控制调度可行性的函数。展示了这些函数的叠加结构，并用递归函数描述多个目标函数。最后制定了递归函数的一般要求并描述了其性质。

Result: 成功构建了能够统一描述六种扩展问题的递归表示框架。通过简单流水车间扩展和分支定界优化的实例，演示了新问题的公式化方法，验证了递归函数框架的实用性和表达能力。

Conclusion: 递归函数为流水车间调度问题的扩展提供了统一而强大的表示框架，能够简洁地描述多种复杂约束和目标函数，为调度问题的建模和优化提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: The paper discusses several extensions of the recursive representation of the flow shop scheduling problem. It is shown that recursive functions make it possible to describe multiple extensions in a single problem. The paper considers altogether six extensions. The examples consider three types of recursive functions: functions associated with the machine, functions that adjust the procession time based on constraints, and functions that control the feasibility of the schedule. The structure of the superpositions of these functions is presented, and also descriptions of several objective functions by recursive functions are presented. Then the general requirements for a recursive function are formulated and its properties are described. Finally, a demonstration of the formulation of new problems is provided using examples of simple flow shop extensions and branch and bound optimization.

</details>


### [117] [Asymptotic Convergence of the Frank-Wolfe Algorithm for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12104)
*Matthew Hough*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文分析了Frank-Wolfe算法在单调算子下求解变分不等式的收敛性，通过连续时间插值和动力系统理论证明了算法的收敛性，并解决了Hammond关于广义虚拟博弈收敛性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Frank-Wolfe算法在单调算子下求解变分不等式的收敛性，特别是针对广义虚拟博弈等应用场景。需要建立离散迭代与连续时间动力系统之间的联系，以分析算法的渐近行为。

Method: 引入离散迭代的连续时间插值，利用动力系统理论分析其渐近行为。研究在单调C¹算子和消失、非可求和的步长条件下，Frank-Wolfe算法的收敛特性。

Result: 证明了Frank-Wolfe算法在单调算子下的收敛性：每个迭代的聚点都是变分不等式的解；迭代到解集的距离收敛到零；Frank-Wolfe间隙渐近消失。在强单调情况下，解唯一且迭代收敛到该解。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了Frank-Wolfe算法在单调算子下的收敛理论，通过连续时间分析方法解决了离散迭代的收敛问题，特别证明了Hammond关于广义虚拟博弈收敛性的猜想。

Abstract: We consider the Frank-Wolfe algorithm for solving variational inequalities over compact, convex sets under a monotone $C^1$ operator and vanishing, nonsummable step sizes. We introduce a continuous-time interpolation of the discrete iteration and use tools from dynamical systems theory to analyze its asymptotic behavior. This allows us to derive convergence results for the original discrete algorithm. Consequently, every cluster point of the iterates is a solution of the underlying variational inequality, the distance from the iterates to the solution set converges to zero, and the Frank-Wolfe gap vanishes asymptotically. In the strongly monotone case, the solution is unique and the iterates converge to it. In particular, this proves Hammond's conjecture on the convergence of generalized fictitious play.

</details>


### [118] [Folding Mixed-Integer Linear Programs and Reflection Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12136)
*Rolf van der Hulst*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了DRCR方法，将其应用于反射对称性和混合整数线性规划，通过仿射全幺模分解减少整数变量，实验证明该方法能有效处理对称性并减少求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性对分支定界和线性优化算法的性能有负面影响，传统的DRCR方法仅处理置换对称性，需要扩展到更一般的对称类型和混合整数规划问题。

Method: 扩展DRCR方法：1) 将DRCR扩展到反射对称性；2) 应用于混合整数线性规划的连续变量；3) 使用仿射全幺模分解重新表述混合整数规划以减少整数变量数量。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017数据集上使用SCIP 10的实验表明：对于线性规划松弛，带反射对称性的DRCR比原始DRCR运行时间略有减少；对于混合整数规划模型，DRCR相比SCIP默认配置显著减少求解时间；检测算法快速且可扩展到大规模问题实例。

Conclusion: DRCR是处理对称性的有效工具，扩展后的方法能处理更广泛的对称类型并应用于混合整数规划，检测算法高效且可扩展，为对称性处理提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: For mixed-integer linear programming and linear programming it is well known that symmetries can have a negative impact on the performance of branch-and-bound and linear optimization algorithms. A common strategy to handle symmetries in linear programs is to reduce the dimension of the linear program by aggregating symmetric variables and solving a linear program of reduced dimension. In their work ``Dimension Reduction via Color Refinement'' (DRCR), Grohe, Kersting, Mladenov and Selman show that it is sufficient to run a fast color refinement algorithm to detect permutation symmetries and reduce the dimension of the linear program. We extend DRCR in two directions. First, we show that DRCR can be extended to reflection symmetries, which generalize permutation symmetries. Second, we show the folklore result that DRCR can be applied to the continuous columns of mixed-integer linear programs.
